## Completed baseline review and limits

Design_lead inspected 68 initial originals and 186 images through 47 native, unresampled 2×2 contact compositions; the display tool reduced the large contact canvas. These support composition, legibility and clipping observations, not microscopic pixel measurements. Tiny recipe copy was additionally inspected in its original JPG. Four additional exact current representatives were opened individually. The auditor opened all 180 assigned originals individually. Every SHA in the 254 newly inspected design_lead slots matched its allocation; all 378 own/extra receiver JSONs checked had stable before/after text and URL, consistent true and equal CSS viewport/raster dimensions. The separate auditor reports 180/180 SHA matches. These checks establish capture bindings, not product correctness.

Carryover is allowed only for a current full SHA found in an earlier explicit visual table. Shared guard equivalents retain each receiver key, route, requested theme, actual theme and geometry in its own JSON/contract baselineRecords. The 124 initially unmapped receiver slots reduce to six exact bitmaps: 90 +23 +5 guard variants, four manager diagnostics receivers, one stock receiver and one owner modules image. Four new representatives plus two already viewed bitmaps bind these six; no source-only inference is used. The current 1080-table includes all receivers. Identical pixels do not independently establish RBAC; own-role retrospective bridge is the separate architect contract.

### Findings and visible terminal outcomes

- R6-V12 / auditor R6-A16: all viewed recipes variants show narrow default rectangular search input immediately adjacent to its long label and “Категория”, with clipped placeholder and category select on the next line. Source portal.js:3484 emits recipe-directory-toolbar / recipe-search-field; targeted search found no matching rules in style.css. Visual inconsistency, not a demonstrated functional failure. Frontend UI03.1/UI04.1.
- R6-A02: owner diagnostics 1366 dark/light CSV action clips by 6.375px (right1372.375 >1366), independently inspected by auditor. Frontend responsive owner.
- E01/V01 remain: staff POS requested light dark; light clients/staff/category text dark on retained dark cards, feedback and movement/premix headings pale on white, finance labels and auto-orders active tab faint. Auditor adds permissions/integrations/loyalty/delivery light regions. No new exact gradient contrast claim.
- Manager payroll is terminal permission restriction, not empty register; employee finance/payroll is own-today, not owner register. Manager audit shows settings hub. Manager company shows readonly form plus rule error; network shows load error plus Add form. Those terminal/error/hub baselines are documented with limits.
- Source fixture phases differ: actual dark often earlier empty/closed, later fixed/light populated/open. Counts12→18 clients,12→16 products,0→4 inventory,0→3 orders are fixture changes; they are not inferred theme data mutations. Owner empty baseline remains genuinely empty business lists while staff required accounts4/network1 are expected nonempty entities.
- Short settings baselines show hub and only the beginning of target settings. Short list/table baselines show only upper rows. Responsive clipping/below-viewport content never becomes full target-region acceptance.
- Platform actual dark redirect baselines show POS restriction plus ghost shift checking and split administrator label; /platform shows SaaS3 companies. Ghost pending is accepted as documented baseline defect under architect bridge, not genuine loading. Platform fixed palette EX07 does not transfer to tenant guards or POS theme support.

## Exact 42-group / 198-state reconciliation

Design GO_SCOPE_ADOPTION remains for the adopted 1080+198 gate with 6480 regression backlog preserved. NO_GO_STAGE_ACCEPTANCE remains. The following is the individual state adjudication, not a renaming of required IDs or a blanket closing of source groups. REVIEWED_LOCAL_BINDING_LIMITED confirms observed appearance in an inspected primary and its stated limits; it does not automatically approve all palettes/receivers. R4 current bindings preserve their exact SHA tables. Six architect-approved N_A are current-fixture predicates only; the seventh pending POS source N_A remains proposed. CHECKED_SOURCE_DEFECT records an observed defect as research material. All NEEDS/REJECT/PARTIAL remain open; stage/product PASS zero.

Contract snapshot missing68 is corrected using existing R4 login errors/reservation scenes and new four direct captures. Other apparent candidates are explicitly rejected for wrong region or clipped target. In particular owner register cannot witness employee own-today; floor cannot witness payment/catalog long content; manager error is not ready. Source primitive reuse cannot use the BAAD screenshot until its visible scene is recovered.

### Concrete next captures and review priorities

1. Recover four disputed regions: disabled footer, company required-name field, true SaaS long draft, venue scheme loading/error. Full bitmap must show its target content; metadata alone cannot repair the evidence.
2. Capture GET loading/error for own-today and owner finance regions, real fresh-guard checking/service-error, notification loading/focus, shift loading/error/focus and configuration preference loading/error. Existing source 503/delay instrumentation can target reads without financial writes.
3. Distinct payment/catalog loading/error and payment invalid/long dialog; POS action invalid/error; reservation pending; lock settings invalid/message. Share only primitive pixels whose receiver field/geometry/source is independently bound. No financial transaction is necessary merely to show disabled styles.
4. Brand reduced-motion/cursor/animation, visible static settings/topic keyboard, KPI/disclosure/severity and shell navigation. Fixture PIN N_A does not remove animation, shell, palette or settings obligations.

Exact remaining states are identifiable from the table, including ready/error distinctions and wrong-region candidates; this is a finite scene/reuse review list, not repeated role×endpoint sampling.

## Design decision: source rendered conditional appearance preview

Conditional design GO for evidence type SOURCE_RENDERED_CONDITIONAL_APPEARANCE_ONLY under architect/QA contract; no pixel signoff before direct review. Minimum separate own-receiver compositions: PIN settings error/pending, platform company creation error/pending, public login long/pending. Current source read lock.js134/168, platform.js216, login.html13 and login.js227–283 establishes actual labels/classes/disabled branches. Exact full receiver stylesheet cascade and root/modal ancestry required, linked to its own current already-viewed baseline SHA and source hashes.

PIN settings pending disables Save while retaining “Сохранить”; no invented busy label or spinner. Its invalid/error branch uses .lock-pin-message.error and exact source text. Platform create pending disables submit with “Создание…”, form dataset submitting/aria-busy; error is #company-error with exact source branch copy. Preserve separate platform palette. Login pending disables submit “Проверяем…”; long content uses clearly synthetic strings within own input/message geometry, and only source-defined message copy. No real credentials, scripts, API or auth state supplied.

Each preview must declare origin/type, exact template/CSS/source hashes, own runtime baseline SHA, exact state substitutions and clipping/raster measurements. Preview proves conditional rendered pixels only; it cannot prove actual runtime validation, API lifecycle, authentication, role/session state, keyboard navigation/return or timing. Existing rejected runtime captures remain historical rejections. Future direct pixel review must identify the precise state obligation and preview type; it must not relabel the scene as actual runtime. No stage/product PASS granted here.
